- **auth**: New and updated managed projects write `[auth] mode = "single"` in `.cland/config.toml`. Missing auth settings also mean single mode. - **away-team**: The Away Team orchestrator. Runs only as the main thread the user selected (Claude Code: claude --agent away-team or /away-team; Copilot: /agent, away-team), never as a subagent. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/away-team.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **away-team-basher**: Fixes a bug from an investigator Diagnosis, or does a small fully-specified change. Failing test first, minimal root-cause fix at the point all callers share, run tests, commit, report evidence or a Blocked report. Does not push or open PRs. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/agents/away-team-basher.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-investigator**: Root-causes a bug quickly, or gives a second opinion on a proposal already on an issue. Read-only. Reproduces, localises, tests one hypothesis at a time, returns a Diagnosis with path:line evidence and a fix recommendation, a Review of what the item and its comments propose, or a Blocked report. Never edits files. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/agents/away-team-investigator.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-mapper**: Trawls a solution and writes docs/CODEMAP.md (entry points, module boundaries, data flow, invariants, verified build/test commands) so investigator and basher can navigate without re-reading the repo. Run once per repo, then refresh. Returns a Map report or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/away-team-mapper.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-pr-writer**: Opens or refreshes a pull request from the current branch in the house format. Conventional-commit title, TL;DR body, full technical breakdown posted as PR review comments. Uses the GitHub MCP server, falling back to gh only where that binary exists. Returns the PR URL or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/agents/away-team-pr-writer.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-reviewer**: Works through the open review threads on a pull request. Applies the small, local ones (nit, rename, one-function change, missing test) with a commit each, replies on every thread, pushes only when the user confirmed, and leaves larger design questions to the author with a proposal. Returns a Revision report or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/agents/away-team-reviewer.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **awcms-coder**: Agent implementasi AWCMS. Dipakai oleh orchestrator untuk mengerjakan satu issue/sprint AWCMS secara atomic end-to-end (kode, migration, OpenAPI/AsyncAPI, test, docs, changeset) ketika user minta "kerjakan issue ([ahliweb/awcms](https://github.com/ahliweb/awcms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/awcms-coder.md) · ★14 · 3 copies)
- **awcms-reviewer**: Agent review PR/diff AWCMS (read-only). Gunakan untuk mereview pull request, diff branch, atau hasil kerja awcms-coder terhadap Definition of Done AWCMS. Tidak mengubah kode — hanya menganalisis dan melaporkan temuan. ([ahliweb/awcms](https://github.com/ahliweb/awcms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/awcms-reviewer.md) · ★14 · 3 copies)
- **awcms-security-auditor**: Agent audit keamanan modul AWCMS (read-only). Gunakan untuk security review sebuah modul/perubahan sensitif (auth, tax, sync, POS posting, CRM) atau menjelang go-live. Menghasilkan temuan berperingkat; critical finding memblokir go-live. ([ahliweb/awcms](https://github.com/ahliweb/awcms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/awcms-security-auditor.md) · ★14 · 3 copies)
- **awesome-cobol_agents**: You are operating within `awesome-cobol`, a static curation repository consisting entirely of Markdown documentation (100% composition). ([squid-protocol/gitgalaxy](https://github.com/squid-protocol/gitgalaxy/blob/HEAD/docs/wiki/agents/awesome-cobol_agents.md) · ★67)

- **awf-lookup**: awf 查詢員：用 awf 的唯讀子指令與讀檔把資料查出來、整理好再回報，主對話只拿結論。使用者問「這個 run 卡在哪／為什麼停了／下一步要下什麼」（診斷）、「核准前幫我整理重點／這一段要我決定什麼」（閘門摘要）、「錢花在哪／哪個角色最花 token」（成本）、「交付文件哪些過期、還缺什麼」（交付盤點）、「設定有沒有問題／doctor 的警告是什麼意思」（設定體檢），或「某個需求在文件與程式的哪裡、改了會影響什麼」「把相關的規格、決定、紀錄整理成摘要」時主動派它。只讀：不改檔、不核准、不推送、不跑 workflow。 ([s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow](https://github.com/s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/awf-lookup.md) · ★1)
- **awf-refresh**: awf 更新員：把專案的衍生資料更新到最新——重建知識庫索引（awf kb build）、更新知識圖（awf kg build）、重寫交付文件總目錄（awf deliverables index），並回報更新了什麼、還有哪些要人處理。適合「文件改了很多，索引要重建」「知識圖舊了」「交付文件的目錄沒跟上」這類耗時、輸出很長、主對話只需要結果的工作。只能跑這幾個固定的更新指令，其餘唯讀：不改專案的檔、不核准、不推送、不跑 workflow。 ([s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow](https://github.com/s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/awf-refresh.md) · ★1)
